# combined_de.txt — German translations for Pokémon Unbound (EN→DE)
#
# Same format as combined_fr.txt:  <offset_hex>: <text_DE>
#   \n  -> line break        \l -> <0xFA> (scroll)      \p -> <0xFB> (clear)
# Lines starting with '#' and blank lines are ignored.
# Offsets absent from this file keep their English text.
# Last entry wins for a duplicated offset (case-insensitive).
#
# STATUS: seed / work in progress. These entries prove the EN→DE pipeline
# end to end; the bulk of the script still needs translating.
#
# ä ö ü Ä Ö Ü are now fully encodable (charmap slots 0x63-0x60, glyphs drawn
# by scripts/patch_font_de.py). Use real umlauts below — no ae/oe/ue needed.
# ß is at 0x15 and has a ROM glyph; use it directly.
#
# --- seed entries (verified against the English extraction) ---
